- **visualize**: Turn a plan, report, dataset, or explanation into a self-contained visual artifact - a single offline HTML file (default) or a PDF. Use on demand when a wall of markdown is hard to read, when a plan or spec needs reviewing before approval, when data needs a chart or dashboard, or when the deliverable must be a PDF. Triggers on 'visualize', 'visualise', 'render this as HTML', 'make an HTML page', 'make a report page', 'turn this into a page', 'make a visual', 'chart this', 'dashboard for', 'PDF of this', 'export to PDF', 'make this readable'.
